5
Conclusion and Extensions

This paper develops a compound decision approach for estimating many binomial parameters by working directly with the exact binomial distribution. We construct an approximate Stein’s unbiased risk estimator for the average mean squared error that remains valid under heterogeneous sample sizes and small counts, without relying on Gaussian approximations. For a class of machine-learning–assisted linear shrinkage estimators, we establish asymptotic optimality, regret bounds relative to the oracle, and valid inference. The estimators satisfy a reporting-consistency property and are guaranteed to improve upon the maximum likelihood estimator and any single predictive model under mild conditions. More broadly, the framework offers a computationally tractable method for combining information pooling with covariate adjustment across large collections of binomial problems. Empirical applications to discrimination detection, education outcomes, and innovation rates show that the proposed estimators deliver stable and interpretable gains in accuracy in settings with heterogeneous sample sizes, small samples, or small binomial means.

For future work, we plan to incorporate Bayes estimators into the shrinkage framework and to extend the analysis to settings with selection decisions
[
8
]
, which will require a more sophisticated bias calculation for our risk estimator. In addition, if most
n_{i}
are much larger than
2
, we can also add higher-order polynomials of
Y_{i}
as covariates.

Appendix A
Additional Proofs for Section
2

A.1
Additional Proofs for Section
2

Proof of Proposition
2.1
.

By definition,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[(n-Y)g(Y+1)]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}(n-i)g(i+1)\frac{n!}{i!(n-i)!}\theta^{i}(1-\theta)^{n-i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}g(i+1)\frac{n!}{i!(n-i-1)!}\theta^{i}(1-\theta)^{n-i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}(i+1)g(i+1)\frac{n!}{(i+1)!(n-i-1)!}\theta^{i}(1-\theta)^{n-i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\theta}{\theta}\sum_{j=1}^{n}jg(j)\frac{n!}{j!(n-j)!}\theta^{j}(1-\theta)^{n-j}

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\theta}{\theta}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[Yg(Y)].

∎

Recall that
for any function
h
on
\{0,\ldots,n\}
,

\mathcal{T}_{1}h(y;n):=\mathbf{1}(y>0)\sum_{j=0}^{n-y}h(y+j)(-1)^{j}\frac{(n-y)!}{(n-y-j)!}\frac{y!}{(y+j)!},

\mathcal{T}_{2}h(y;n):=h(y)-\mathbf{1}(y<n)\sum_{j=0}^{y}h(y-j)(-1)^{j}\frac{y!}{(y-j)!}\frac{(n-y)!}{(n-y+j)!},

\Delta h:=\sum_{j=0}^{n}h(j)(-1)^{j}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
j\end{array}\right).

Proof of Theorem
2.1
.

Note that we can rewrite
\mathcal{T}_{1}h
and
\mathcal{T}_{2}h
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{1}h(y;n)

\displaystyle=(-1)^{y}\mathbf{1}(y>0)\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
y\end{array}\right)^{-1}\sum_{k=y}^{n}h(k)(-1)^{k}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
k\end{array}\right),

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{2}h(y;n)

\displaystyle=h(y)-(-1)^{y}\mathbf{1}(y<n)\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
y\end{array}\right)^{-1}\sum_{k=0}^{y}h(k)(-1)^{k}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
k\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=h(n)\mathbf{1}(y=n)-(-1)^{y}\mathbf{1}(y<n)\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
y\end{array}\right)^{-1}\sum_{k=0}^{y-1}h(k)(-1)^{k}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
k\end{array}\right)

Recall the definition of
\Delta h
, we have

\mathcal{T}_{1}h(y;n)-\mathcal{T}_{2}h(y;n)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}0&y=0\text{ or }n\\
(-1)^{y}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
y\end{array}\right)^{-1}(\Delta h)&0<y<n\end{array}\right.

Then

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[(\mathcal{T}_{1}h(Y;n)-\mathcal{T}_{2}h(Y;n))\mathbf{1}(Y>a)]=(\Delta h)\sum_{y=a+1}^{n-1}(-1)^{y}\theta^{y}(1-\theta)^{n-y}.

As a result,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\mathcal{T}h(Y;n,a)]-\theta\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[h(Y)]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\mathcal{T}_{2}h(Y;n)]-\theta\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[h(Y)]+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[(\mathcal{T}_{1}h(Y;n)-\mathcal{T}_{2}h(Y;n))\mathbf{1}(Y>a)]

\displaystyle=-(1-\theta)\theta^{n}(-1)^{n+1}(\Delta h)+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[(\mathcal{T}_{1}h(Y;n)-\mathcal{T}_{2}h(Y;n))\mathbf{1}(Y>a)]

\displaystyle=(\Delta h)\left\{-(1-\theta)\theta^{n}(-1)^{n+1}+\sum_{y=a+1}^{n-1}(-1)^{y}\theta^{y}(1-\theta)^{n-y}\right\}

\displaystyle=(\Delta h)\left\{-(1-\theta)\theta^{n}(-1)^{n+1}+(1-\theta)^{n}\sum_{y=a+1}^{n-1}\left(\frac{-\theta}{1-\theta}\right)^{y}\right\}

\displaystyle=(\Delta h)\left\{-(1-\theta)\theta^{n}(-1)^{n+1}+(1-\theta)^{n}\cdot\left(\frac{-\theta}{1-\theta}\right)^{a+1}\frac{1-\left(\frac{-\theta}{1-\theta}\right)^{n-a-1}}{1+\frac{\theta}{1-\theta}}\right\}

\displaystyle=(\Delta h)(-1)^{a+1}\theta^{a+1}(1-\theta)^{n-a}.

Hence (
11
) follows.

Next, to show statement (i) of Theorem
2.1
, note that (
11
) implies that

\left|\theta\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[h(Y)]-\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\mathcal{T}h(Y;n,\lfloor n/2\rfloor)]\right|=\left|\theta^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor+1}(1-\theta)^{n-\lfloor n/2\rfloor}(\Delta h)\right|.

(34)

On the one hand, when
n=2k
where
k\geq 1
, then

\left|\theta^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor+1}(1-\theta)^{n-\lfloor n/2\rfloor}(\Delta h)\right|=|\theta^{k+1}(1-\theta)^{k}(\Delta h)|\leq{4}^{-k}|\Delta h|=2^{-n}|\Delta h|,

where we use the fact that
\theta(1-\theta)\leq 1/4
in the inequality above. On the other hand, when
n=2k-1
where
k\geq 1
, then
\lfloor n/2\rfloor=k-1
, and

\left|\theta^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor+1}(1-\theta)^{n-\lfloor n/2\rfloor}(\Delta h)\right|=|\theta^{k}(1-\theta)^{k}(\Delta h)|\leq{4}^{-k}|\Delta h|=2^{-n}|\Delta h|.

Lastly, to show statement (ii) of Theorem
2.1
, note that for any
x\in[0,1]
,

(1-x)^{n}=\sum_{j=0}^{n}x^{j}(-1)^{j}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
j\end{array}\right).

For any
r\in\{0,\ldots,n-1\}
, taking
r
-th derivatives with respect to
x
yields that

\frac{n!}{(n-r)!}(1-x)^{n-r}=\sum_{j=r}^{n}\frac{j!}{(j-r)!}x^{j-r}(-1)^{j}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
j\end{array}\right).

Letting
x=1
, we obtain that for any
r\in\{0,\ldots,n-1\}
,

\sum_{j=r}^{n}\frac{j!}{(j-r)!}(-1)^{j}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
j\end{array}\right)=0.

Clearly, given any polynomial
h
of degree less than
n
, there exist real numbers
w_{r}
for
r\in\{0,1,\ldots,n-1\}
, such that for every integer
j\in\{0,1,\ldots,n\}
,

h(j)=\sum_{r=0}^{n-1}w_{r}\frac{j!}{(j-r)!}.

Consequently,

\Delta h=\sum_{j=0}^{n}h(j)(-1)^{j}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
j\end{array}\right)=\sum_{r=0}^{n-1}w_{r}\sum_{j=r}^{n}\frac{j!}{(j-r)!}(-1)^{j}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}n\\
j\end{array}\right)=0.

Therefore,
\Delta h=0
when
h
is a polynomial of degree less than
n
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
2.3
.

For any
i\in[N]
, recall that
k(i)
is the fold that the
i
-th sample belongs to. Given any
k\neq k(i)
, for any
j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}
, define
\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\cdot)
as the ML estimator for
g(\cdot)
computed on the dataset excluding fold
k
and replacing
Y_{i}
with its independent copy
Y_{i}^{(2)}
. Define

\bar{Y}_{-i}:=\frac{\sum_{j=1,j\neq i}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}.

Define

\begin{array}[]{rl}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})&\displaystyle:=\lambda_{1}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}+(1-\lambda_{1})\bar{Y}_{-i}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\lambda_{2}\left(\hat{g}^{-k(i)}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k(i)}}n_{j}\hat{g}^{-k(i)}(\mathbf{X}_{j})+\sum_{k\neq k(i)}^{K}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}n_{j}\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right).\end{array}

Thus by definition

\begin{array}[]{rl}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})-\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})&\displaystyle=\frac{(1-\lambda_{1})Y_{i}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}-\frac{\lambda_{2}\sum_{k\neq k(i)}^{K}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}n_{j}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\end{array}

(35)

Define the term on the right hand side of (
35
) as

\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda}):=\frac{(1-\lambda_{1})Y_{i}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}-\frac{\lambda_{2}\sum_{k\neq k(i)}^{K}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}n_{j}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}.

(36)

So

\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})=\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda}).

(37)

For any
i\in[N]
, define

\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}:=\{Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{i-1},Y_{i+1},\ldots,Y_{N}\},

so
\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}
is the collection of one-sample observations excluding the
i
-th observation. Note that
Y_{i}\ \raisebox{0.50003pt}{\rotatebox[origin]{90.0}{$\models$}}\ \hat{g}^{-k(i)}(\mathbf{X}_{j})
and
Y_{i}\ \raisebox{0.50003pt}{\rotatebox[origin]{90.0}{$\models$}}\ \hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})
for any
k\neq k(i)
. Conditioning on
\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}
,
\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})
is affine in
Y_{i}
. So when
n_{i}\geq 2
, Theorem
2.1
implies that

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]=\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right].

(38)

Furthermore, using definition of
\mathcal{T}_{1}
(
7
), and recall that when we apply functional
\mathcal{T}
to
\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})
, we fix the grand mean
(\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i})/(\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i})
and the ML model outputs
\hat{g}^{-k(j)}(\mathbf{X}_{j})
,
\forall j\in[N]
. Thus

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathcal{T}_{1}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})&\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}_{1}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\cdot\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}>0\}\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i}-Y_{i}}(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i}-Y_{i})!}{(n_{i}-Y_{i}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i}!}{(Y_{i}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle=_{(1)}\mathcal{T}_{1}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}>0\}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda}),\end{array}

(39)

where equality (1) of (
39
) follows directly from Lemma
D.5
. Similarly, using definition of
\mathcal{T}_{2}
(
8
), we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathcal{T}_{2}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})&\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}_{2}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})-\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\cdot\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}<n_{i})\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i}}(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i}!}{(Y_{i}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i}-Y_{i})!}{(n_{i}-Y_{i}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle=_{(2)}\mathcal{T}_{2}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}<n_{i}\}\left[1-\frac{n_{i}-Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\right]\\
&\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}_{2}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}<n_{i}\}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda}).\end{array}

(40)

So following from (
10
),

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathcal{T}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})&\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\\
&\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})+\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda}).\end{array}

(41)

Then (
41
) implies that

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]&\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]\\
&\displaystyle=_{(1)}\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]\\
&\displaystyle=_{(2)}\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})-\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]\\
&\displaystyle=\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right\}\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right],\end{array}

(42)

where in (
42
), (1) follows from (
38
), (2) follows from (
37
). Hence, using the fact that
|Y_{i}/n_{i}-\theta_{i}|\in[0,1]
, we have

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]-\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]\right|\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\right|\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]

(43)

We now bound the right hand side of (
43
).
Since
2\leq n_{j}\leq\bar{n}
for any
j\in[N]
,

\left|\frac{(1-\lambda_{1})Y_{i}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right|\leq\frac{\bar{n}}{2N}.

Note that
\hat{g}^{-k}
and
\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}
are both between
0
and
1
, and Assumption
2.1
(b) implies that given any
i\in[N]
, for any
k\neq k(i)
,
\max_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-g(\mathbf{X}_{j})|=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)
, so using dominated convergence theorem, we have
\max_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-g(\mathbf{X}_{j})|\right]=\mathrm{o}(1)
. Since
\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}
is computed by replacing
Y_{i}
with its independent copy
Y_{i}^{(2)}
and
\mathbf{X}_{j}
are treated as fixed, so by definition, for any
k\neq k(i)
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-g(\mathbf{X}_{j})|\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-g(\mathbf{X}_{j})|\right],

implying that
\max_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\mathbb{E}[|\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-g(\mathbf{X}_{j})|]=\mathrm{o}(1).

Thus by triangular inequality,

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\max_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k},k\neq k(i)}\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})|\right]\\
&\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k},k\neq k(i)}\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-g(\mathbf{X}_{j})|\right]+\max_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k},k\neq k(i)}\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-g(\mathbf{X}_{j})|\right]\\
&\displaystyle=o(1).\end{array}

So we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\lambda_{2}\sum_{k\neq k(i)}^{K}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}n_{j}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right|\right]\\
&\displaystyle\leq\frac{|\lambda_{2}|\sum_{k\neq k(i)}^{K}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}n_{j}\mathbb{E}[|\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})-\hat{g}_{i}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{j})|]}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}=\mathrm{o}(1)\end{array}

Hence, (
36
) implies that

\mathbb{E}[|\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})|]\leq\frac{\bar{n}}{2N}+\mathrm{o}(1).

(44)

Thus (
43
) and (
44
) imply that

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\right]-\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\right]\right|&\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]-\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]\right\}\right|\\
&\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]-\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]\right|\right\}\\
&\displaystyle\leq_{(1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\right|\big|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right]\right]\\
&\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\right]\leq_{(2)}\frac{\bar{n}}{2N}+\mathrm{o}(1),\end{array}

(45)

where in (
45
) (1) follows from (
43
) and (2) follows from (
44
). The upper bound in (
45
) immediately implies that

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{L}_{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\right]-L_{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})\right|\leq\frac{\bar{n}}{N}+\mathrm{o}(1).

For the two-sample estimator, note that
\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})=\hat{\theta}_{i1}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})-\hat{\theta}_{i2}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})
. Define

\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{t}}:=\{Y_{i\ell},\ldots,Y_{i-1,\ell},Y_{i+1,\ell},\ldots,Y_{N\ell}\}_{\ell\in\{1,2\}},

so
\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{t}}
is the two-sample observations excluding the
i
-th pair of observations
\{Y_{i1},Y_{i2}\}
. Following similar proof steps as for the one-sample case, we can show that there exists some absolute constant
\bar{C}
, such that almost surely we have

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(Y_{i1};n_{i1}|\bm{\lambda})|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{t}},Y_{i2}\right]-\theta_{i1}^{\mathrm{t}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{t}},Y_{i2}\right]\right|\leq\bar{n}/N+\mathrm{o}(1),

and

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{T}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(Y_{i2};n_{i2}|\bm{\lambda})|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{t}},Y_{i1}\right]-\theta_{i2}^{\mathrm{t}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})|\mathbf{Y}_{-i}^{\mathrm{t}},Y_{i1}\right]\right|\leq\bar{n}/N+\mathrm{o}(1),

Hence by definition almost surely we have

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{L}_{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})\right]-L_{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})\right|\leq 4\bar{n}/N+\mathrm{o}(1).

Hence we have proved the results.
∎

A.2
Explicit Expressions for Approximate SUREs

A.2.1
One-Sample Approximate SURE

Denote

\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{(1)}:=\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(Y_{i};n_{i})^{2}-2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}>0)\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i}-Y_{i}}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(Y_{i}+j;n_{i})(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i}-Y_{i})!}{(n_{i}-Y_{i}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i}!}{(Y_{i}+j)!},

(46)

and

\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{(2)}:=\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(Y_{i};n_{i})^{2}-2\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(Y_{i};n_{i})+2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}<n_{i})\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i}}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(Y_{i}-j;n_{i})(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i}!}{(Y_{i}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i}-Y_{i})!}{(n_{i}-Y_{i}+j)!}.

(47)

Expanding (
21
), the explicit expression for the one-sample approximate SURE is

\hat{L}_{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(Y_{i};n_{i})^{2}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{(1)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{(2)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}.

(48)

A.2.2
Two-Sample Approximate SURE

Define
\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{1,(1)}
and
\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{1,(2)}
as

\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{1,(1)}:=2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i1}>0)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i1}-Y_{i1}}\!\!\!\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(Y_{i1}+j;n_{i1}|\bm{\lambda})(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1})!}{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i1}!}{(Y_{i1}+j)!},

(49)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{1,(2)}&\displaystyle:=2\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(Y_{i1};n_{i1}|\bm{\lambda})\\
&\quad\displaystyle-2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i1}<{n_{i1}})\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i1}}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(Y_{i1}-j;n_{i1}|\bm{\lambda})(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i1}!}{(Y_{i1}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1})!}{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1}+j)!}.\end{array}

(50)

Define
\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{2,(1)}
and
\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{2,(2)}
as

\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{2,(1)}:=2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i2}>0)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i2}-Y_{i2}}\!\!\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(Y_{i2}+j;n_{i2}|\bm{\lambda})(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2})!}{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i2}!}{(Y_{i2}+j)!},

(51)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{2,(2)}&\displaystyle:=2\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(Y_{i2};n_{i2}|\bm{\lambda})\\
&\quad\displaystyle-2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i2}<{n_{i2}})\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i2}}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(Y_{i2}-j;n_{i2}|\bm{\lambda})(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i2}!}{(Y_{i2}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2})!}{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2}+j)!}.\end{array}

(52)

Expanding (
22
), the explicit expression for the two-sample approximate SURE is

\begin{array}[]{rl}\hat{L}_{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})&=\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})^{2}-\frac{1}{N}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{1,(1)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i1}>\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor\}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{1,(2)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i1}\leq\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor\}\right]\\
\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{N}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{2,(1)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i2}>\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor\}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\hat{\ell}_{i,\mathrm{u}}^{2,(2)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i2}\leq\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor\}\right].\end{array}

(53)

Appendix B
Proofs for Quadratic Forms of Objectives Functions

Proof of Proposition
3.1
.

The proposition follows directly according to Lemma
B.1
, Lemma
B.2
, Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
.
∎

B.1
Lemmas of Quadratic Function Forms for One-Sample Case

Define

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&\displaystyle\bar{Y}:=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{N}Y_{k}}{\sum_{k=1}^{N}n_{k}},\ \bar{\theta}:=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{N}n_{k}\theta_{k}}{\sum_{k=1}^{N}n_{k}},&\\
\\
&\displaystyle g_{i}=g(\mathbf{X}_{i}):=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\,\right|\,\mathbf{X}_{i}\right],\ \hat{g}:=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{N}n_{k}\hat{g}_{k}(X_{k})}{\sum_{k=1}^{N}n_{k}},\ \bar{g}:=\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}g(\mathbf{X}_{i})}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}.&\end{array}

(54)

\bm{\beta}_{i}:=\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}},\hat{g}_{i}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}\hat{g}_{j}(\mathbf{X}_{j})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right)^{T},

\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}+j):=\left(\frac{Y_{i}+j}{n_{i}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}},\hat{g}_{i}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}\hat{g}_{j}(\mathbf{X}_{j})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right)^{T},

\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}-j):=\left(\frac{Y_{i}-j}{n_{i}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}},\hat{g}_{i}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}\hat{g}_{j}(\mathbf{X}_{j})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right)^{T}.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\overline{\bm{\beta}}_{i}=\overline{\bm{\beta}}_{i}(Y_{i}):&=&\displaystyle\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\bar{\theta},g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\bar{g}\right)^{T},\\
\overline{\bm{\beta}}_{i}(Y_{i}+j):&=&\displaystyle\left(\frac{Y_{i}+j}{n_{i}}-\bar{\theta},g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\bar{g}\right)^{T},\\
\overline{\bm{\beta}}_{i}(Y_{i}-j):&=&\displaystyle\left(\frac{Y_{i}-j}{n_{i}}-\bar{\theta},g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\bar{g}\right)^{T},\end{array}

where
\displaystyle\frac{Y_{i}+j}{n_{i}},\frac{Y_{i}-j}{n_{i}},g_{i},\hat{g}_{i},\bar{g},\hat{g}\in[0,1]
. Let
\mathbf{\Delta}_{i}=(\bar{\theta}-\bar{Y},\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})
, then

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\bm{\beta}_{i}&=&\overline{\bm{\beta}}_{i}+\mathbf{\Delta}_{i}\\
\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}+j)&=&\overline{\bm{\beta}}_{i}(Y_{i}+j)+\mathbf{\Delta}_{i}\\
\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}-j)&=&\overline{\bm{\beta}}_{i}(Y_{i}-j)+\mathbf{\Delta}_{i}.\end{array}

(55)

It is easy to see that
\max\{\|\bm{\bar{\beta}}_{i}\|_{\infty},\|\bm{\bar{\beta}}_{i}(Y_{i}+j)\|_{\infty},\|\bm{\bar{\beta}}_{i}(Y_{i}-j)\|_{\infty}\}\leq 2
.

Lemma B.1
.

\hat{L}_{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})=\bm{\lambda}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{N,2}\bm{\lambda}+\mathbf{C}_{N,1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}+\mathbf{C}_{0}
, where
\bm{\lambda}=(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})^{T}
,
C_{0}
is a constant not related to
\bm{\lambda}
, and

\mathbf{C}_{N,2}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\beta}_{i}\bm{\beta}_{i}^{T},

(56)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathbf{C}_{N,1}&\displaystyle=\frac{2\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\left\{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\beta}_{i}\right\}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i}-Y_{i}}\!\!\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}+j)(-1)^{j}\frac{\binom{n_{i}-Y_{i}}{j}}{\binom{Y_{i}+j}{j}}\\
&\quad\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\bm{\beta}_{i}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor)\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i}}\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}-j)(-1)^{j}\frac{\binom{Y_{i}}{j}}{\binom{n_{i}-Y_{i}+j}{j}}.\end{array}

(57)

Proof of Lemma
B.1
.

Let
\bm{\lambda}=(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})^{T}
,
According to (
48
), the one-sample SURE using estimator (
14
) can be written as

\begin{array}[]{rl}\hat{L}_{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\bm{\beta}_{i}^{T}\bm{\lambda}+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right)^{2}-\hat{\ell}_{i,u}^{(1)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}-\hat{\ell}_{i,u}^{(2)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\displaystyle=\bm{\lambda}^{T}\left[\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\beta}_{i}\bm{\beta}_{i}^{T}\right]\bm{\lambda}+\frac{2\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\bm{\lambda}^{T}\left\{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\beta}_{i}\right\}+\left(\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right)^{2}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor)\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i}-Y_{i}}\!\!\!\left(\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}+j)^{T}\bm{\lambda}+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right)(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i}-Y_{i})!}{(n_{i}-Y_{i}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i}!}{(Y_{i}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\left(\bm{\beta}_{i}^{T}\bm{\lambda}+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right)\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor)\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i}}\left(\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}-j)^{T}\bm{\lambda}+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\right)(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i}!}{(Y_{i}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i}-Y_{i})!}{(n_{i}-Y_{i}+j)!},\end{array}

(58)

so the coefficient for quadratic term is

\mathbf{C}_{N,2}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\beta}_{i}\bm{\beta}_{i}^{T},

(59)

and the coefficient for the first-order term is

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathbf{C}_{N,1}&\displaystyle=\frac{2\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\left\{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\beta}_{i}\right\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i}-Y_{i}}\!\!\!\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}+j)(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i}-Y_{i})!}{(n_{i}-Y_{i}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i}!}{(Y_{i}+j)!}\\
&\quad\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\bm{\beta}_{i}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\quad\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor)\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i}}\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}-j)(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i}!}{(Y_{i}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i}-Y_{i})!}{(n_{i}-Y_{i}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{2\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j}}\left\{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\beta}_{i}\right\}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i}-Y_{i}}\!\!\!\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}+j)(-1)^{j}\frac{\binom{n_{i}-Y_{i}}{j}}{\binom{Y_{i}+j}{j}}\\
&\quad\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\bm{\beta}_{i}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i}\leq\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor)\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i}}\bm{\beta}_{i}(Y_{i}-j)(-1)^{j}\frac{\binom{Y_{i}}{j}}{\binom{n_{i}-Y_{i}+j}{j}}\end{array}

(60)

∎

Lemma B.2
.

L_{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})=\bm{\lambda}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{2}\bm{\lambda}+\mathbf{C}_{1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}+C_{0}^{*}
, where
\bm{\lambda}=(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})^{T}
,
C_{0}^{*}
is a constant not related to
\bm{\lambda}
, and

\mathbf{C}_{2}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\beta}_{i}\bm{\beta}_{i}^{T}\right],

(61)

\mathbf{C}_{1}=\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\bar{Y}-\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right)\bm{\beta}_{i}\right].

(62)

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.

The lemma is straightforward to prove by expanding (
19
) with the definition of
\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})
defined as (
16
).
∎

B.2
Lemmas of Quadratic Function Forms for Two-Sample Case

Denote

\bm{\beta}_{i1}:=\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{Y_{i1}}{n_{i1}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j1}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}}\\
\displaystyle\hat{g}_{i1}(\mathbf{X}_{i1})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}\hat{g}_{j1}(\mathbf{X}_{j1})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}}\end{pmatrix},\ \bm{\beta}_{i2}:=\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{Y_{i2}}{n_{i2}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j2}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}\\
\displaystyle\hat{g}_{i2}(\mathbf{X}_{i2})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}\hat{g}_{j2}(\mathbf{X}_{j2})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}\end{pmatrix},

\bm{\beta}_{i1}(Y_{i1}+j):=\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{Y_{i1}+j}{n_{i1}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j1}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}}\\
\displaystyle\hat{g}_{i1}(\mathbf{X}_{i1})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}\hat{g}_{j1}(\mathbf{X}_{j1})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}}\end{pmatrix},\ \bm{\beta}_{i2}(Y_{i2}+j):=\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{Y_{i2}+j}{n_{i2}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j2}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}\\
\displaystyle\hat{g}_{2}(\mathbf{X}_{i2})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}\hat{g}_{j2}(\mathbf{X}_{j2})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}\end{pmatrix},

\bm{\beta}_{i1}(Y_{i1}-j):=\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{Y_{i1}-j}{n_{i1}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j1}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}}\\
\displaystyle\hat{g}_{1}(\mathbf{X}_{i1})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}\hat{g}_{j1}(\mathbf{X}_{j1})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}}\end{pmatrix},\ \bm{\beta}_{i2}(Y_{i2}-j):=\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{Y_{i2}-j}{n_{i2}}-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j2}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}\\
\displaystyle\hat{g}_{2}(\mathbf{X}_{i2})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}\hat{g}_{j2}(\mathbf{X}_{j2})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}\end{pmatrix}.

\bar{Y}_{1}=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j1}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}},\quad\bar{Y}_{2}=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j2}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}.

(63)

\bar{Y}_{1}=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j1}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}},\ \bar{Y}_{2}=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}Y_{j2}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}},\ \bar{\theta}_{1}=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}\theta_{j1}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}},\ \bar{\theta}_{2}=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}\theta_{j2}}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}},

g_{1i}:=g_{1}(\mathbf{X}_{i})=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i1}}{n_{i1}}\bigg|\mathbf{X}_{i1}\right],\ \bar{g}_{1}=\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i1}g_{1}(\mathbf{X}_{i})}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i1}},\ \hat{g}_{1}=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}\hat{g}_{1}(\mathbf{X}_{j1})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}}.

g_{2i}:=g_{2}(\mathbf{X}_{i})=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i2}}{n_{i2}}\bigg|\mathbf{X}_{i2}\right],\ \bar{g}_{2}=\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i2}g_{2}(\mathbf{X}_{i})}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i2}},\ \hat{g}_{2}=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}\hat{g}_{2}(\mathbf{X}_{j2})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}.

\Delta\hat{g}_{i}=\left[\hat{g}_{i1}(\mathbf{X}_{i1})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}\hat{g}_{j1}(\mathbf{X}_{j1})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j1}}\right]-\left[\hat{g}_{i2}(\mathbf{X}_{i2})-\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}\hat{g}_{j2}(\mathbf{X}_{j2})}{\sum_{j=1}^{N}n_{j2}}\right].

Lemma B.3
.

\hat{L}_{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})=\bm{\lambda}^{T}\mathbf{D}_{N,2}\bm{\lambda}+\mathbf{D}_{N,1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}+D_{0}
, where
\bm{\lambda}=(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})^{T}
,
D_{0}
is a constant matrix not related to
\bm{\lambda}
,

\mathbf{D}_{N,2}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T},

(64)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathbf{D}_{N,1}&\displaystyle=2(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\left\{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})\right\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i1}>\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i1}-Y_{i1}}\!\!\!(\bm{\beta}_{i1}(Y_{i1}+j)-\bm{\beta}_{i2})(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1})!}{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i1}!}{(Y_{i1}+j)!}\\
&\quad\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i1}\leq\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\quad\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i1}\leq\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor)\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i1}}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}(Y_{i1}-j)-\bm{\beta}_{i2})(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i1}!}{(Y_{i1}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1})!}{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i2}>\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i2}-Y_{i2}}\!\!\!(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2}(Y_{i2}+j))(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2})!}{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i2}!}{(Y_{i2}+j)!}\\
&\quad\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i2}\leq\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\quad\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i2}\leq\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor)\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i2}}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2}(Y_{i2}-j))(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i2}!}{(Y_{i2}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2})!}{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2}+j)!}.\end{array}

(65)

Proof of Lemma
B.3
.

Let
\bm{\lambda}=(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})^{T}
,
According to (
53
),

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\hat{L}_{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left((\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T}\bm{\lambda}+\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2}\right)^{2}-\hat{\ell}_{i,u}^{1,(1)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i1}>\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor\}-\hat{\ell}_{i,u}^{1,(2)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i1}\leq\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\displaystyle+\hat{\ell}_{i,u}^{2,(1)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i2}>\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor\}+\hat{\ell}_{i,u}^{2,(2)}\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i2}\leq\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\displaystyle=\bm{\lambda}^{T}\left[\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T}\right]\bm{\lambda}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+2(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\bm{\lambda}^{T}\left\{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})\right\}+(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})^{2}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i1}>\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor)\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i1}-Y_{i1}}\!\!\!\left((\bm{\beta}_{i1}(Y_{i1}+j)-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T}\bm{\lambda}+(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\right)(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1})!}{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i1}!}{(Y_{i1}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\left((\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T}\bm{\lambda}+(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\right)\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i1}\leq\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i1}\leq\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor)\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i1}}\left((\bm{\beta}_{i1}(Y_{i1}-j)-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T}\bm{\lambda}+(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\right)(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i1}!}{(Y_{i1}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1})!}{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i2}>\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor)\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i2}-Y_{i2}}\!\!\!\left((\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2}(Y_{i2}+j))^{T}\bm{\lambda}+(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\right)(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2})!}{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i2}!}{(Y_{i2}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\left((\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T}\bm{\lambda}+(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\right)\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i2}\leq\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i2}\leq\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor)\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i2}}\left((\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2}(Y_{i2}-j))^{T}\bm{\lambda}+(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\right)(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i2}!}{(Y_{i2}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2})!}{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2}+j)!}\end{array}

(66)

So the coefficient for the quadratic term is

\mathbf{D}_{N,2}:=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T},

(67)

and the coefficient for the first-order term is

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathbf{D}_{N,1}&\displaystyle=2(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})\left\{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})\right\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i1}>\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i1}-Y_{i1}}\!\!\!(\bm{\beta}_{i1}(Y_{i1}+j)-\bm{\beta}_{i2})(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1})!}{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i1}!}{(Y_{i1}+j)!}\\
&\quad\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i1}\leq\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\quad\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i1}\leq\lfloor n_{i1}/2\rfloor)\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i1}}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}(Y_{i1}-j)-\bm{\beta}_{i2})(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i1}!}{(Y_{i1}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1})!}{(n_{i1}-Y_{i1}+j)!}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i2}>\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor)\!\!\!\sum_{j=0}^{n_{i2}-Y_{i2}}\!\!\!(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2}(Y_{i2}+j))(-1)^{j}\frac{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2})!}{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2}-j)!}\frac{Y_{i2}!}{(Y_{i2}+j)!}\\
&\quad\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i2}\leq\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor\}\\
&\quad\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}2\mathbf{1}(Y_{i2}\leq\lfloor n_{i2}/2\rfloor)\sum_{j=0}^{Y_{i2}}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2}(Y_{i2}-j))(-1)^{j}\frac{Y_{i2}!}{(Y_{i2}-j)!}\frac{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2})!}{(n_{i2}-Y_{i2}+j)!}.\end{array}

(68)

∎

Lemma B.4
.

L_{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})=\bm{\lambda}^{T}\mathbf{D}_{2}\bm{\lambda}+\mathbf{D}_{1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}+D_{0}^{*}
, where
\bm{\lambda}=(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})^{T}
,
D_{0}^{*}
is a constant not related to
\bm{\lambda}
, and

\mathbf{D}_{2}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})^{T}\right],

(69)

\mathbf{D}_{1}=\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{(\bar{Y}_{1}-\bar{Y}_{2})-(\theta_{i1}^{\mathrm{t}}-\theta_{i2}^{\mathrm{t}})\right\}(\bm{\beta}_{i1}-\bm{\beta}_{i2})\right].

(70)

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.

The lemma is straightforward to prove by expanding (
20
) with the definition of
\hat{\theta}_{i}^{\mathrm{t}}(\bm{\lambda})=\hat{\theta}_{i1}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})-\hat{\theta}_{i2}^{\mathrm{o}}(\bm{\lambda})
defined as (
18
).
∎

Appendix C
Proofs for Asymptotic Normality

3.1

3.1
follows directly from Theorem
C.1
and Theorem
C.2
.
∎

C.1
Proofs for Asymptotic Normality of One-Sample Case

Assumption C.1
.

Suppose the following statements hold:

(i)
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{Var}(Y_{i})\rightarrow\sigma_{Y}^{2}
,
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}]\rightarrow\mu_{Y}^{*}
,
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}\rightarrow\mu_{n}^{*}
,
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\rightarrow\mu_{gn}^{*}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\rightarrow\mu_{\theta}^{*}
,
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\theta_{i}^{\mathrm{o}}\right)^{2}\rightarrow\sigma_{\theta}^{2}
,
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\rightarrow\mu_{g}^{*}
,
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}\right]\rightarrow\mu_{I}^{*}
, where
\sigma_{Y}^{2}
,
\sigma_{gn}^{2}
,
\mu_{Y}^{*}
,
\mu_{n}^{*}
,
\mu_{gn}^{*}
,
\mu_{\theta}^{*}
,
\sigma_{\theta}
,
\mu_{g}^{*}
,
\mu_{I}^{*}
are all absolute constants.

(ii)
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{Cov}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}\right\}\rightarrow\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}
, where
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\in\mathbb{R}^{6\times 6}
is a positive definite matrix and

\tilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}:=(\tilde{\zeta}_{i1},\tilde{\zeta}_{i2},\tilde{\zeta}_{i3},\tilde{\zeta}_{i4},\tilde{\zeta}_{i5},\tilde{\zeta}_{i6})^{T}\in\mathbb{R}^{6},\quad i\in[N]

are i.n.i.d. vectors defined as (
71
):

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&\displaystyle\Delta_{i}^{Y}:=Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}],&\\
&\displaystyle\Delta_{i}^{gn}:=n_{i}g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\mathbb{E}[n_{i}g(\mathbf{X}_{i})],&\\
&\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}_{i1}=4\frac{\left(\mu_{\theta}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\Delta_{i}^{Y}+2\left\{\frac{Y_{i}/n_{i}(1-Y_{i}/n_{i})}{n_{i}-1}-\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)^{2}\right\},&\\
&\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}_{i2}=2\frac{\left(\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\Delta_{i}^{Y}-2\frac{\left(\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}-\mu_{I}^{*}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\Delta_{i}^{gn}+2\left(\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}\right)\left(g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right),&\\
&\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}_{i3}=2\frac{\left(\mu_{\theta}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\Delta_{i}^{Y}+\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)^{2},&\\
&\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}_{i4}=\tilde{\zeta}_{i5}=\frac{\left(\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\Delta_{i}^{Y}+\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\left(g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right),&\\
&\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}_{i6}=2\frac{\left(\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}-\mu_{g}^{*}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\Delta_{i}^{gn}+\left(g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)^{2}.&\end{array}

(71)

Theorem C.1
.

Suppose Assumption
2.1
and Assumption
C.1
hold. Suppose
\bm{\lambda}_{\mathrm{o}}^{*}
is unconstrained. Then

\sqrt{N}\left(\hat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\mathrm{o}}-\bm{\lambda}_{\mathrm{o}}^{*}\right)\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{V}),

where
\mathbf{V}\preceq\bar{C}\mathbf{I}_{2}
for some absolute constant
\bar{C}
.

Proof of Theorem
C.1
.

Recall that
\bm{\mu}_{m,1}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{C}_{N,1}]
and
\bm{\mu}_{m,2}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{C}_{N,2}]
. According to Lemma
C.1
we have

\sqrt{N}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{N,1}-\bm{\mu}_{m,1}\\
\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{C}_{N,2}-\bm{\mu}_{m,2})\end{pmatrix}\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right).

Define

g(c_{1},c_{2})=-\frac{1}{2}c_{2}^{-1}c_{1}

for
c_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2}
,
c_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{2\times 1}
. So

g(\bm{\mu}_{m,1},\bm{\mu}_{m,2})=-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{m,2}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{m,1}.

For perturbations
(h_{1},h_{2})\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2}
at
(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2}
, denote
\bm{\lambda}=\mu_{2}^{-1}\mu_{1}
, and using the fact that
\mathrm{vec}(ABC)=(C^{T}\otimes A)\mathrm{vec}(B)
, then we have

Dg_{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}(h_{1},h_{2})=\frac{1}{2}\mu_{2}^{-1}h_{1}-\frac{1}{2}\mu_{2}^{-1}h_{2}\mu_{2}^{-1}\mu_{1}=\frac{1}{2}\mu_{2}^{-1}h_{1}-\frac{1}{2}(\bm{\lambda}^{T}\otimes\mu_{2}^{-1})\mathrm{vec}(h_{2}).

Hence the
2\times 6
Jacobian at
(\bm{\mu}_{m,1},\bm{\mu}_{m,2})
that multiplies the stacked vector
(h_{1},\mathrm{vec}(h_{2}))^{T}
is

\mathbf{J}=\begin{bmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{m,2}^{-1}&\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\bm{\mu}_{m,1}^{T}\bm{\mu}_{m,2}^{-1}\otimes\bm{\mu}_{m,2}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}.

Applying delta’s method, we then have

\sqrt{N}\left(\hat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\mathrm{o}}-\left\{-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{m,2}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{m,1}\right\}\right)\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{J}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\mathbf{J}^{T}\right),

where
\mathbf{V}=\mathbf{J}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\mathbf{J}^{T}\preceq\bar{C}\mathbf{I}_{2}
for some absolute constant
\bar{C}
. So Theorem
C.1
follows from Lemma
C.2
that

\sqrt{N}\left(\bm{\lambda}_{\mathrm{o}}^{*}-\left\{-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{m,2}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{m,1}\right\}\right)=\begin{pmatrix}b_{1}\\
b_{2}\end{pmatrix},

(72)

where
b_{1}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)
and
b_{2}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)
.
∎

C.1.1
Technical Lemmas for the One-Sample Asymptotic Normality Result

Lemma C.1
.

Let
\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{C}_{N,2})
be the vector with the four column-wise entries stacked,
\bm{\mu}_{m,1}:=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{C}_{N,1}]
and
\bm{\mu}_{m,2}:=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{C}_{N,2}]
. Suppose Assumption
2.1
holds, then

\sqrt{N}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{N,1}-\bm{\mu}_{m,1}\\
\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{C}_{N,2}-\bm{\mu}_{m,2})\end{pmatrix}\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right),

(73)

where
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\preceq\bar{c}\mathbf{I}_{6}
for some absolute constant
\bar{c}
, and
\mathbf{I}_{6}
is the
6
-by-
6
identity matrix.

Proof of Lemma
C.1
.

For notational convenience, rewriting

\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{N,1}\\
\mathrm{Vec}(\mathbf{C}_{N,1})\end{bmatrix}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\xi}_{i},

(74)

where
\bm{\xi}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{6}
and is the
i
-th summand vector related to
\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{N,1}\\
\mathrm{Vec}(\mathbf{C}_{N,1})\end{bmatrix}
. Let
\mathbf{Z}_{i}=(n_{i},Y_{i},X_{i})
, which are independent across
i
but not necessarily identically distributed (i.n.i.d.). Denote

p_{i}:=\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}},\quad v_{i}:=\frac{p_{i}(1-p_{i})}{n_{i}-1},

and we denote the population-level constants as

\bar{Y}^{*}:=\frac{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}]}{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}},\quad\bar{\theta}^{*}:=\frac{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}\theta_{i}}{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}.

Denote the oracle vector
\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}
by freezing global average quantities at population-level values and removing the cross-fitted estimators
\hat{g}_{i},\hat{g}
, where

\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}:=\begin{pmatrix}\displaystyle 2\left\{v_{i}-\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\bar{Y}^{*}\right)^{2}\right\}\\
\displaystyle 2\left(\bar{Y}^{*}-\mathbb{I}_{i}^{+}\right)\left(g_{i}-\bar{g}\right)\\
\displaystyle\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\bar{Y}^{*}\right)^{2}\\
\displaystyle\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\bar{Y}^{*}\right)(g_{i}-\bar{g})\\
\displaystyle\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\bar{Y}^{*}\right)(g_{i}-\bar{g})\\
\displaystyle(g_{i}-\bar{g})^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}.

(75)

Then
\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}
are independent across
i\in[N]
, and

\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\bm{\xi}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{\xi}_{i}]\right\}=\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}-\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}]+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}]\right\},

(76)

where
\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}=(\Delta_{N1},\Delta_{N2},\Delta_{N3},\Delta_{N4},\Delta_{N5},\Delta_{N6})^{T}
, and

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\Delta_{N1}&=&\!\!\!\!\displaystyle(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})\left[\frac{4}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-2(\bar{Y}+\bar{Y}^{*})\right]\\
\\
\Delta_{N2}&=&\!\!\!\!\displaystyle 2(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(g_{i}-\bar{g})+\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\bar{Y}-\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\right)(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\\
\\
\Delta_{N3}&=&\!\!\!\!\displaystyle(\bar{Y}^{*}-\bar{Y})\left[\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-(\bar{Y}+\bar{Y}^{*})\right]\\
\\
\Delta_{N4}&=&\!\!\!\!\displaystyle(\bar{Y}^{*}-\bar{Y})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(g_{i}-\bar{g})-\bar{Y}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\\
\\
\Delta_{N5}&=&\!\!\!\!\displaystyle(\bar{Y}^{*}-\bar{Y})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(g_{i}-\bar{g})-\bar{Y}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\\
\\
\Delta_{N6}&=&\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})^{2}-(g_{i}-\bar{g})^{2}\}\end{array}

(77)

Firstly, note that

\sqrt{N}\left(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*}\right)=\frac{\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])}{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}.

(78)

Note that under (iii) of Assumption
2.1
,
\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{Var}(Y_{i})\rightarrow\sigma_{Y}^{2}
. Under (iii) of Assumption
2.1
,
\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}\rightarrow\mu_{n}^{*}
. Further note that
\{Y_{i}\}_{i\in[N]}
are independent across
i\in[N]
. Hence according to (
78
), using Slutsky’s theorem and Lindeberg-Feller
Central Limit Theorem
(Lemma
D.7
), we have

\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}(0,\bar{\sigma}_{Y}^{2}),

(79)

where
\bar{\sigma}_{Y}
is a constant.

Secondly, note that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})-\mathbb{E}[\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g}]\}\\
&\displaystyle=-\sqrt{N}\left[(\hat{g}-\bar{g})-\mathbb{E}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\right]+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{(\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))-\mathbb{E}[(\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))]\right\},\end{array}

(80)

where

\begin{array}[]{rl}\sqrt{N}\left(\hat{g}-\bar{g}\right)&\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sqrt{N}n_{i}\{\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}n_{i}\sqrt{N}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{(N/K)\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{(N/K)}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}n_{i}\sqrt{N}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}n_{i}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}}{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}.\end{array}

(81)

Note that

\sqrt{N}\mathbb{E}\left[(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\right]-\frac{\sqrt{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}[n_{i}\{\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}]}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}=0,

(82)

Hence (
81
) and (
82
) imply that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\sqrt{N}\left[(\hat{g}-\bar{g})-\mathbb{E}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\right]\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\{n_{i}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))-\mathbb{E}[n_{i}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))]\}}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}.\end{array}

(83)

Note that within each fold the samples are independent, hence

\begin{array}[]{rl}\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}n_{i}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}\right]=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\mathrm{Var}[n_{i}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}],\end{array}

where for each
i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[n_{i}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}\right]&\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[n_{i}^{2}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}^{2}\right]\\
&\displaystyle\leq\bar{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\}^{2}\right]=_{(1)}\mathrm{o}(1),\end{array}

where (1) follows from Assumption
2.1
. Thus

\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\{n_{i}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))-\mathbb{E}[n_{i}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))]\}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).

(84)

Recall that
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mu_{n}^{*}
, hence according to (
81
) and (
83
), we have

\sqrt{N}\{(\hat{g}-\bar{g})-\mathbb{E}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).

(85)

Thirdly, note that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\mathbb{E}[\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})]\}\\
&\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\mathbb{E}[\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})]\}=_{(b)}\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),\end{array}

(86)

where (b) holds following similar proving steps for (
84
). So according to (
80
), (
85
) and (
86
) we have

\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})-\mathbb{E}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)

(87)

Fourthly, note that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\sqrt{N}\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\bar{Y}-\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\right)(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\\
&\displaystyle=2\bar{Y}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})-\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\\
&\displaystyle=2\bar{Y}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})-\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i})+2\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\\
&\displaystyle=2\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})+2\bar{Y}^{*}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i})+2\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}.\end{array}

(88)

Note that
\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})=\mathrm{O}_{p}(1)
according to (
79
), and according to (ii) of Assumption
2.1
we have
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)
, hence

2\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).

(89)

Note that
\displaystyle\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})
is uniformly integrable by noting that

|\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g}|\leq 2

and

\sup_{N}\mathbb{E}[|\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})|]\leq\sup_{N}\mathbb{E}[|\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})|^{2}]^{1/2}<\infty,

so (
89
) also implies that

\mathbb{E}\left[\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\right]=\mathrm{o}(1)

(90)

Additionally, note that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))\right]\right\}\\
&\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\left\{\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))\right]\right\},\end{array}

(91)

where for each
k\in[K]
,
\displaystyle\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))
are independent across
i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)
, so

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\left\{\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))\right]\right\}\right]\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\mathrm{Var}\left\{\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))\right\}\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))\right|^{2}\right]\\
&\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\right|^{2}\right]=\mathrm{o}(1).\end{array}

(92)

where the last inequality of (
92
) uses the fact that
0\leq Y_{i}/n_{i}\leq 1
. Hence

-\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}(\hat{g}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i}))\right]\right\}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).

(93)

Further, note that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\\
&=\displaystyle\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\theta_{i}\right)+\left\{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\theta_{i}\right\}\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})=\mu_{\theta}^{*}\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})+\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),\end{array}

(94)

where the last equality above follows since
\displaystyle\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\theta_{i}\right)=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)
and
\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\theta_{i}\rightarrow\mu_{\theta}^{*}
according to (iii) of Assumption
2.1
. Also note that
|\hat{g}-\bar{g}|\leq 1
and

\sup_{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\theta_{i}\right)\right|\right]\leq\sup_{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\theta_{i}\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1/2}<\infty,

thus
\displaystyle\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\theta_{i}\right)
is uniformly integrable, so

\mathbb{E}\left[\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\theta_{i}\right)\right]=\mathrm{o}(1).

Thus according to (
85
) and (
94
) we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\displaystyle\quad\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\mathbb{E}\left[\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\right]\\
&\displaystyle=\mu_{\theta}^{*}\left[\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})-\mathbb{E}\{\sqrt{N}(\hat{g}-\bar{g})\}\right]+\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)\\
&\displaystyle=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).\end{array}

(95)

Hence according to (
88
), (
89
), (
90
), (
93
), (
95
), (
87
), and following (iii) of Assumption
2.1
we have
\displaystyle\bar{Y}^{*}\rightarrow\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}
as
N\rightarrow\infty
, so we have

\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\bar{Y}-\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\right)(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\bar{Y}-\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\right)(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})\right]=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).

(96)

Lastly, note that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})^{2}-(g_{i}-\bar{g})^{2}\}\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}[(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})+(g_{i}-\bar{g})][\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}+g_{i})[\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]-(\hat{g}+\bar{g})\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}[\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]\\
&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}+g_{i}-2\bar{g})[\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]+(\bar{g}-\hat{g})\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}[\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]\\
&\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}(\hat{g}_{i}+g_{i}-2\bar{g})[\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+(\bar{g}-\hat{g})\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\sum_{i=1}^{N}[\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]\end{array}

(97)

Following similar proof steps as those for (
91
), (
92
) and (
93
), we have

\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}(\hat{g}_{i}+g_{i}-2\bar{g})[\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]\right]=\mathrm{o}(1),

and note that
|\bar{g}-\hat{g}|\leq 1
, so

\mathrm{Var}\left[(\bar{g}-\hat{g})\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\sum_{i=1}^{N}[\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]\right]=\mathrm{o}(1),

thus (
97
) implies that

\begin{array}[]{rl}\displaystyle\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}[(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})+(g_{i}-\bar{g})][\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}]\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})+(g_{i}-\bar{g})\}\{\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g}+\bar{g}-g_{i}\}\right].\end{array}

(98)

Hence, (
97
) and (
98
) imply that

\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})^{2}-(g_{i}-\bar{g})^{2}\}-\mathbb{E}\left[(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})^{2}-(g_{i}-\bar{g})^{2}\right]=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).

(99)

Recall from (
74
) and (
76
) that

\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{N,1}\\
\mathrm{Vec}(\mathbf{C}_{N,1})\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{C}_{N,1}]\\
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Vec}(\mathbf{C}_{N,1})\right]\end{bmatrix}=\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}-\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}]+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}]\right\},

where
\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}
are i.n.i.d. across
i\in[N]
. Given any
\mathbf{t}=(t_{1},t_{2},t_{3},t_{4},t_{5},t_{6})\in\mathbb{R}^{6}
, according to (
76
) and (
77
), we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\quad\displaystyle\mathbf{t}^{T}\left\{\sqrt{N}\left\{\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}-\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}]\right\}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}]\right\}\right\}\\
&\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}-\bar{Y}^{*})\Bigg\{t_{1}\left[\frac{4}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-2(\bar{Y}+\bar{Y}^{*})\right]+\frac{2t_{2}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(g_{i}-\bar{g})\\
&\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\ +t_{3}\left[\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-(\bar{Y}+\bar{Y}^{*})\right]+\frac{t_{4}+t_{5}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(g_{i}-\bar{g})\Bigg\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+t_{2}\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\bar{Y}-\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\right)(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})-2\sqrt{N}(\bar{g}-\bar{g})t_{2}\left[\bar{Y}^{*}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{I}_{i}^{+}\right]\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\bar{Y}(t_{4}+t_{5})\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})+\frac{t_{6}}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})^{2}-(g_{i}-\bar{g})^{2}\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{t}^{T}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}]\right\}-\mathbf{t}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\sqrt{N}\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}]+\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).\end{array}

Using (iii) of Assumption
2.1
, Law of Large Numbers, Slutsky’s theorem, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mu_{\theta}^{*},\ \displaystyle\bar{Y}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}},\ \displaystyle\bar{Y}^{*}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}},\ \displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(g_{i}-\bar{g})\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}},\ \displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{I}_{i}^{+}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mu_{I}^{*}.

Then we have

Z_{N}(t)-\mathbf{t}^{T}\left\{\sqrt{N}\left\{\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}-\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}]\right\}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}]\right\}\right\}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),

(100)

where

\begin{array}[]{rl}Z_{N}(t)&\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}(\bar{Y}\!-\!\bar{Y}^{*})\left\{\left(4\mu_{\theta}^{*}\!-\!4\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\!t_{1}\!+\!\left(2\mu_{\theta}^{*}\!-\!2\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\!t_{3}\!+\!(2t_{2}\!+\!t_{4}\!+\!t_{5})\!\left(\mu_{g}^{*}\!-\!\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\right\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-(t_{4}+t_{5})\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{(\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g})-\mathbb{E}[\hat{g}_{i}-g_{i}+\bar{g}-\hat{g}]\}\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{t_{6}}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\{(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})^{2}-(g_{i}-\bar{g})^{2}\}-\mathbb{E}[(\hat{g}_{i}-\hat{g})^{2}-(g_{i}-\bar{g})^{2}]\right)\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{t}^{T}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}]\right\}\\
&=\displaystyle\mathbf{t}^{T}\bm{\zeta}_{N}^{\prime}=\mathbf{t}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\zeta_{N1}^{\prime}\\
\zeta_{N2}^{\prime}\\
\zeta_{N3}^{\prime}\\
\zeta_{N4}^{\prime}\\
\zeta_{N5}^{\prime}\\
\zeta_{N6}^{\prime}\end{pmatrix},\end{array}

(101)

where

\zeta_{N1}^{\prime}=4\left(\mu_{\theta}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\frac{\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i1}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i1}]\},

\begin{array}[]{rl}\zeta_{N2}^{\prime}&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i2}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i2}]\}+2\left(\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\frac{\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}},\end{array}

\zeta_{N3}^{\prime}=2\left(\mu_{\theta}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\frac{\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i3}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i3}]\},

\begin{array}[]{rl}\zeta_{N4}^{\prime}=\zeta_{N5}^{\prime}&\displaystyle=\left(\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\frac{\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])}{\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\!\!\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\left[\left(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\right)-\mathbb{E}[\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})]\right]\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})}{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}\right]-\frac{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})}{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}n_{i}}\right]\\
&\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i4}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i4}]\}.\end{array}

\zeta_{N6}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i6}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i6}]\}+\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),

(102)

where (

) follows from (

). Using (iii) of Assumption

, Law of Large Numbers and Slutsky’s theorem, we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&\zeta_{N1}^{\prime}-\zeta_{N1}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),\ \zeta_{N2}^{\prime}-\zeta_{N2}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),\ \zeta_{N3}^{\prime}-\zeta_{N3}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),\ \zeta_{N4}^{\prime}-\zeta_{N4}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1)&\\
&\zeta_{N5}^{\prime}-\zeta_{N5}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),\ \zeta_{N6}^{\prime}-\zeta_{N6}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),&\end{array}

(103)

where

\zeta_{N1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left[4\frac{\left(\mu_{\theta}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])+\left\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i1}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i1}]\right\}\right],

(104)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\displaystyle\zeta_{N2}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Bigg[&\displaystyle 2\frac{\left(\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])+\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i2}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i2}]\}\Bigg],\end{array}

\zeta_{N3}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left[2\frac{\left(\mu_{\theta}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])+\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i3}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i3}]\}\right],

(106)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\zeta_{N4}=\zeta_{N5}&\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left[\frac{\left(\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])+\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i4}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i4}]\}\right]\\
&\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\left\{\left(\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\right]\right\},\end{array}

(107)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\displaystyle\zeta_{N6}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i6}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i6}]\},\end{array}

(108)

Thus according to (
75
), (
100
), (

), (

), (

), (
105
), (
106
), (
107
), (
108
), given any
\mathbf{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{6}
, using (iii) of Assumption
2.1
and Slutsky’s theorem, we have

\mathbf{t}^{T}\left\{\sqrt{N}\left\{\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}-\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{\Delta}_{N}]\right\}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\xi}}_{i}]\right\}\right\}-\mathbf{t}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{N}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1),

(109)

where

\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{N}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\{\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}]\}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\tilde{\bm{\delta}}_{i},

(110)

\tilde{\bm{\delta}}_{i}=\begin{pmatrix}0\\
0\\
0\\
\displaystyle(\mu_{Y}^{*}/\mu_{n}^{*})\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\mathbb{E}[\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})]\}\\
\displaystyle(\mu_{Y}^{*}/\mu_{n}^{*})\{\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\mathbb{E}[\hat{g}^{-k}(\mathbf{X}_{i})-g(\mathbf{X}_{i})]\}\\
0\end{pmatrix},

and

\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}=(\tilde{\zeta}_{i1},\tilde{\zeta}_{i2},\tilde{\zeta}_{i3},\tilde{\zeta}_{i4},\tilde{\zeta}_{i5},\tilde{\zeta}_{i6})^{T},

such that
\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}
are i.n.i.d. across
i\in[N]
. According to (

), we have

\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i\in\textrm{Fold}(k)}\tilde{\bm{\delta}}_{i}\right\|_{\infty}=\mathrm{o}_{p}(1).

(111)

Each entry of
\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}
is defined as

\tilde{\zeta}_{i1}=4\frac{\left(\mu_{\theta}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\left\{Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}]\right\}+2\left\{\frac{Y_{i}/n_{i}(1-Y_{i}/n_{i})}{n_{i}-1}-\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)^{2}\right\},

\begin{array}[]{rl}\tilde{\zeta}_{i2}&\displaystyle=2\frac{\left(\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])+2\left(\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}-\mathbf{1}\{Y_{i}>\lfloor n_{i}/2\rfloor\}\right)\left(g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right),\end{array}

\tilde{\zeta}_{i3}=2\frac{\left(\mu_{\theta}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])+\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)^{2},

\tilde{\zeta}_{i4}=\tilde{\zeta}_{i5}=\frac{\left(\mu_{g}^{*}-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)}{\mu_{n}^{*}}(Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}])+\left(\frac{Y_{i}}{n_{i}}-\frac{\mu_{Y}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)\left(g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right),

\tilde{\zeta}_{i6}=\left(g(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\frac{\mu_{gn}^{*}}{\mu_{n}^{*}}\right)^{2}.

According to (iv) of Assumption
2.1
, we have

\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathrm{Cov}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}\right\}\rightarrow\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}.

Note that according to (i) of Assumption
2.1
, and the fact that
Y_{i}/n_{i},g(\mathbf{X}_{i})\in[0,1]
, then
|\tilde{\zeta}_{i\ell}|\leq c_{0}
for some absolute constant
c_{0}
, hence there must exists an absolute constant
\bar{c}
, such that

\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\preceq\bar{c}\mathbf{I}_{6},

where
\mathbf{I}_{6}
is the
6
-by-
6
identity matrix. Additionally, note that

\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}\mathbf{1}\left\{\left\|\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}\right]\right\|_{2}>\sqrt{N}\epsilon\right\}\right]\rightarrow 0,\ \ \forall\epsilon>0.

Recall that
\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}
are independent across
i\in[N]
. Hence by Lemma
D.7
, we have

\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}\right]\right\}\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right).

(112)

Thus, according to (
74
), (
76
), (
109
), (
110
), (
111
), (
112
), given any
\mathbf{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{6}
, we have

\mathbf{t}^{T}\sqrt{N}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{N,1}-\bm{\mu}_{m,1}\\
\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{C}_{N,2}-\bm{\mu}_{m,2})\end{pmatrix}\rightsquigarrow\mathcal{N}\left(0,\mathbf{t}^{T}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\mathbf{t}\right).

Thus (
73
) holds using Cramer-Wold theorem (Lemma
